127: def review_subject(repository: MaintainPlanRepository, subject_ref: str,
128:                    *, now: datetime | None = None) -> CoachReview:
129:     """Inspect every still-unmapped artifact without modifying the repository.
130: 
131:     Corrupt storage is deliberately allowed to raise. Ambiguous and empty scopes
132:     are returned without a write: absence in imported data is not non-completion.
133:     """
134:     mappings = repository.list_prescription_mappings()
135:     from .rpe_feedback import session_rpe_state
136:     rpe_requests = tuple(
137:         (mapping.actual_session_ref, session_rpe_state(repository, mapping.actual_session_ref))
138:         for mapping in mappings
139:         if (repository.get_actual_session(mapping.actual_session_ref) is not None and
140:             repository.get_actual_session(mapping.actual_session_ref).subject_ref == subject_ref)
141:     )
142:     snapshots_all = repository.list_prescription_snapshots(subject_ref)
143:     sessions_all = repository.list_actual_sessions(subject_ref)
144:     snapshots_by_id = {item.prescription_snapshot_id: item for item in snapshots_all}
145:     sessions_by_id = {item.session_id: item for item in sessions_all}
146:     # A mapping is committed before its evaluation. Recover that second step on
147:     # every later visit, making a process interruption safe and retryable.
148:     suspended = []
149:     completed = []
150:     for mapping in mappings:
151:         snapshot = snapshots_by_id.get(mapping.prescription_snapshot_ref)
152:         session = sessions_by_id.get(mapping.actual_session_ref)
153:         if snapshot is None or session is None:
154:             continue
155:         existing_evaluation = repository.get_execution_evaluation_by_mapping(
156:             mapping.mapping_id, applicable_policy=EXECUTION)
157:         if existing_evaluation is not None:
158:             completed.append((CandidatePair(
159:                 snapshot.prescription_snapshot_id, session.session_id),
160:                 existing_evaluation))
--- righe 167-240 ---
167:             suspended.append((pair,
168:                 "Valutazione non completata: usa l’azione esplicita per riprovare."))
169:             continue
170:     mapped_prescriptions = {item.prescription_snapshot_ref for item in mappings}
171:     mapped_sessions = {item.actual_session_ref for item in mappings}
172:     snapshots = tuple(item for item in snapshots_all
173:                       if item.prescription_snapshot_id not in mapped_prescriptions)
174:     sessions = tuple(item for item in sessions_all
175:                      if item.session_id not in mapped_sessions and
176:                      repository.get_session_relation(item.session_id) != ("AUTONOMOUS", None))

178:     decision = decide_runtime_matching(scope)
179:     details = _details(decision, snapshots, sessions)
180:     if decision.status is not DecisionStatus.MATCHED or decision.selected_pair is None:
181:         return CoachReview(decision, candidate_details=details,
182:                            suspended_evaluations=tuple(suspended),
183:                            completed_evaluations=tuple(completed),
184:                            rpe_requests=rpe_requests)
185: 
186:     pair = decision.selected_pair
187:     session = next(item for item in sessions if item.session_id == pair.session_id)

190:             "Abbinamento automatico sospeso: l’attività contiene dati in conflitto."))

192:                        suspended_evaluations=tuple(suspended),
193:                        completed_evaluations=tuple(completed),
194:                        rpe_requests=rpe_requests)

308: def _existing_repository(database_path: str | Path) -> MaintainPlanRepository:
309:     path = Path(database_path)
310:     if not path.is_file():
311:         raise FileNotFoundError(
312:             f"Archivio MAINTAIN_PLAN configurato non trovato: {path}")
313:     return MaintainPlanRepository(path)

316: def retry_saved_evaluation(repository: MaintainPlanRepository, subject_ref: str,
317:                            prescription_id: str, session_id: str, *,
318:                            now: datetime | None = None) -> CoachReview:
319:     """Explicitly retry one already-saved mapping without creating a new one."""
320:     mapping = next((item for item in repository.list_prescription_mappings()
321:                     if item.prescription_snapshot_ref == prescription_id
322:                     and item.actual_session_ref == session_id), None)
323:     snapshot = repository.get_prescription_snapshot(prescription_id)
324:     session = repository.get_actual_session(session_id)
325:     if (mapping is None or snapshot is None or session is None or
326:             snapshot.subject_ref != subject_ref or session.subject_ref != subject_ref):
327:         raise ValueError("collegamento da valutare non disponibile per l’atleta")
328:     scope = validate_runtime_matching_scope(subject_ref, (), ())
329:     decision = decide_runtime_matching(scope)
330:     evaluation, pair, message = _evaluate_saved(
331:         repository, snapshot, session, mapping, now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
332:     from .rpe_feedback import session_rpe_state
333:     return CoachReview(decision, evaluation, saved_pair=pair,
334:                        evaluation_message=message,
335:                        rpe_requests=((session.session_id,
336:                                       session_rpe_state(repository, session.session_id)),))
337:

341:     return retry_saved_evaluation(

142:     return tuple(notes)

144: 
145: def _readonly_archive_sessions(archive_path: str | Path,
146:                                subject_ref: str) -> tuple[ActualSession, ...]:
147:     """Decode archive sessions through a query-only connection, without migrations."""
148:     path = Path(archive_path).resolve()
149:     if not path.is_file():
150:         raise FileNotFoundError(f"Archivio MAINTAIN_PLAN non trovato: {path}")
151:     # Even a SQLite read-only connection may create or update -shm for a WAL
152:     # database. Read a private byte-for-byte snapshot of the database and its
153:     # existing WAL sidecars instead, so recovery and shared memory stay private.
154:     with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="ironcoach-archive-read-") as directory:
155:         snapshot = _copy_stable_archive(path, directory)
156:         connection = sqlite3.connect(snapshot)
157:         connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
158:         try:
159:             connection.execute("PRAGMA query_only = ON")
160:             columns = {row[1] for row in connection.execute(
161:                 "PRAGMA table_info(maintain_plan_actual_sessions)")}
162:             required = {"session_id", "start", "composition", "contract_version",
163:                         "payload_schema_version", "payload_json"}
164:             if not required <= columns:
165:                 raise ValueError("schema storico privo della tabella attività compatibile")
166:             if "subject_ref" in columns:
167:                 # Filter ownership before payload decoding: corrupt data belonging to
168:                 # another athlete must not poison this athlete's read-only journey.
169:                 rows = connection.execute(
170:                     "SELECT * FROM maintain_plan_actual_sessions "
171:                     "WHERE subject_ref = ? ORDER BY session_id", (subject_ref,)).fetchall()
172:             else:
173:                 # Legacy schemas predate the indexed ownership column. Their payload
174:                 # ownership is checked conservatively after decoding below.
175:                 rows = connection.execute(
176:                     "SELECT * FROM maintain_plan_actual_sessions ORDER BY session_id").fetchall()
177:         finally:
178:             connection.close()
179:     sessions = []
180:     for row in rows:
181:         if row["payload_schema_version"] != PAYLOAD_SCHEMA_VERSION:
182:             raise ValueError("versione payload MAINTAIN_PLAN storica non supportata")
183:         # v7 added ownership as a relational column without rewriting legacy
184:         # payloads.  Hydrate that column only for the codec's recognised
185:         # pre-v7 ActualSession shape; modern payloads remain strict.
186:         session = deserialize_contract(
187:             row["payload_json"], ActualSession,
188:             legacy_actual_session_subject_ref=(
189:                 row["subject_ref"] if "subject_ref" in columns else None
190:             ),
191:         )
192:         stored_subject = row["subject_ref"] if "subject_ref" in columns else session.subject_ref
193:         errors = validate_actual_session(session, allow_legacy_subject=stored_subject is None)
194:         if errors:
195:             raise ValueError("; ".join(errors))
196:         metadata = (session.session_id, session.start.isoformat(),
197:                     None if session.composition is None else session.composition.value,
198:                     session.contract_version)
199:         if metadata != (row["session_id"], row["start"], row["composition"],
200:                         row["contract_version"]):
201:             raise ValueError("metadati dell’attività archiviata incoerenti")
202:         # A legacy artifact without ownership cannot safely be attributed.
203:         if stored_subject == subject_ref and session.subject_ref == subject_ref:
204:             sessions.append(session)
205:     return tuple(sessions)
206: 
207: 
208: def available_activities(archive_path: str | Path, subject_ref: str) -> tuple[TrialActivity, ...]:
209:     """Read supported historical activities without writing to the archive."""
210:     supported = {Discipline.SWIM, Discipline.BIKE, Discipline.RUN}
211:     result = []
212:     for session in _readonly_archive_sessions(archive_path, subject_ref):
213:         if session.composition is not Composition.SINGLE or len(session.components) != 1:
214:             # This minimal authoring form creates exactly one planned component;
215:             # offering BRICK/MULTISPORT here would guarantee an unmatchable plan.
216:             continue
217:         sports = tuple(dict.fromkeys(
218:             component.discipline.value for component in session.components
219:             if component.discipline in supported))
220:         if not sports:
221:             continue
222:         sources = sorted({source.source for source in session.source_activities})
223:         result.append(TrialActivity(
224:             session.session_id, session.start, sports,
225:             ", ".join(sources) or "Garmin", _comparison_notes(session, supported)))
226:     return tuple(sorted(result, key=lambda item: (item.start, item.session_id), reverse=True))

251:                          quantity, "minutes", (), _policy("maintain-plan-quantity"),

259:                          EvaluationWindow.WHOLE_BLOCK,
260:                          _policy("maintain-plan-continuous-intensity"), None,
261:                          RecoveryContract(Applicability.NOT_APPLICABLE, None), (),
262:                          _policy("maintain-plan-structure")),)),
263:         DoseContract(Applicability.REQUIRED, _policy("maintain-plan-dose-matrix"),
264:                      "quantity-main", "intensity-main"),
265:     )
266:     value = PrescriptionSnapshot(
267:         f"trial-plan-{identifier}", f"trial-workout-{identifier}",
268:         f"trial-decision-{identifier}", timestamp,
269:         # The selected activity timestamp only scopes the comparison. Targets above
270:         # are exclusively coach-authored and are not inferred from this session.
271:         ScheduledWindow(session.start, session.start, "UTC", False),
272:         Composition.SINGLE, (component,), (),
273:         Objective(ObjectiveEvaluability.CONTEXT_ONLY, None,
274:                   context_text="Scenario di prova ipotetico", policy=PolicyRef(None, None)),
275:         _policy("maintain-plan-matching"), PolicyRef(None, None),
276:         Provenance("coach-hypothetical-trial", timestamp), PrescriptionAudit(None),
277:         subject_ref=subject_ref,
278:     )
279:     errors = validate_prescription(value)
280:     if errors:
281:         raise ValueError("; ".join(errors))
282:     return value

293: def run(open_browser: bool = True, subject_ref: str = DEFAULT_SUBJECT) -> None:
294:     archive = configured_database_path()
295:     trial = trial_database_path(archive)
296:     server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 8765),
297:                                  make_trial_handler(archive, str(trial)))

300:     print(f"Scenario coach ipotetico pronto: {url}")
301:     if open_browser and not os.environ.get("CODESPACE_NAME"):
302:         webbrowser.open(url)
303:     server.serve_forever()

40: def _resolve_subject_alias(subject_ref: str, environment=None) -> str:
41:     if not isinstance(subject_ref, str) or not subject_ref:
42:         return subject_ref
43: 
44:     settings = dict(dotenv_values())
45:     settings.update(os.environ if environment is None else environment)
46: 
47:     canonical = settings.get("IRONCOACH_SUBJECT_REF")
48:     raw_aliases = (
49:         settings.get("IRONCOACH_SUBJECT_ALIAS", ""),
50:         settings.get("IRONCOACH_SUBJECT_ALIASES", ""),
51:     )
52:     aliases = {
53:         item.strip().casefold()
54:         for raw in raw_aliases
55:         if isinstance(raw, str)
56:         for item in raw.split(",")
57:         if item.strip()
58:     }
59: 
60:     if (
61:         isinstance(canonical, str)
62:         and canonical
63:         and subject_ref.casefold() in aliases
64:     ):
65:         return canonical
66: 
67:     return subject_ref

712: def configured_imported_plan_path(
713:     subject_ref: str,
714:     project_root: str | Path | None = None,
715: ) -> Path:
716:     root = Path(project_root) if project_root is not None else Path(__file__).parents[2]
717:     settings = dict(dotenv_values(root / ".env"))
718:     settings.update(os.environ)
719: 
720:     directory = Path(
721:         settings.get("IRONCOACH_IMPORTED_PLAN_DIRECTORY")
722:         or root / "data" / "imported_plans"
723:     )
724: 
725:     if not directory.is_absolute():
726:         directory = root / directory
727: 
728:     filename = hashlib.sha256(
729:         subject_ref.encode("utf-8")
730:     ).hexdigest() + ".json"
731: 
732:     return directory / filename
733: 
734: def configured_database_path(project_root: str | Path | None = None) -> str:
735:     root = Path(project_root) if project_root is not None else Path(__file__).parents[2]
736:     added = []
737:     for name, value in dotenv_values(root / ".env").items():
738:         if value is not None and name not in os.environ:
739:             os.environ[name] = value
740:             added.append(name)
741:     try:
742:         config = get_runtime_config()
743:     finally:
744:         for name in added:
745:             os.environ.pop(name, None)
746:     database = Path(config.maintain_plan_database_path)
747:     if not database.is_absolute():
748:         database = root / database
749:     if not database.is_file():
750:         raise FileNotFoundError(
751:             f"Archivio MAINTAIN_PLAN configurato non trovato: {database}")
752:     return str(database)
753: 
754: 
755: def run(open_browser: bool = True, subject_ref: str | None = None) -> None:
756:     database_path = configured_database_path()
757:     if subject_ref is not None:
758:         subject_ref = _resolve_subject_alias(subject_ref)
759:         readiness = database_review_readiness(database_path, subject_ref)
760:         print(readiness.message())
761:         if not readiness.ready:
762:             raise RuntimeError(readiness.message())
763:     server = ThreadingHTTPServer(
764:         ("127.0.0.1", 8765),
765:         make_handler(database_path, configured_subject=subject_ref))
766:     origin = next(iter(_trusted_origins(server.server_address[1]).values()))
767:     label = ("Revisione coach pronta" if subject_ref is not None
768:              else "Pagina coach pronta per il controllo atleta")
769:     print(f"{label}: {origin}")
770:     if open_browser and not os.environ.get("CODESPACE_NAME"):
771:         webbrowser.open(origin + "/")
772:     server.serve_forever()

17: def request_confirmation(result: MatchingResult, *, confirmation_id: str,
18:                          asked_at: datetime, provenance, question: str | None = None) -> Confirmation:
19:     if result.status is not MatchingStatus.CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED or result.prescription_mapping is not None:
20:         raise ValueError("confirmation can only be requested for an unresolved matching result")
21:     interpretations = [
22:         {"answer_type": ConfirmationAnswerType.SELECT_CANDIDATE.value, "session_id": item}
23:         for item in result.candidate_set
24:     ]
25:     if not result.candidate_set:
26:         interpretations.extend({"answer_type": answer.value} for answer in (
27:             ConfirmationAnswerType.NOT_PERFORMED,
--- righe 44-71 ---
44:          "candidate_session_refs": result.candidate_set}, provenance,
45:         declared_session_refs=result.declared_session_refs,
46:     )
47:     errors = validate_confirmation(value)
48:     if errors:
49:         raise ValueError("; ".join(errors))
50:     return value

52: 
53: def answer_confirmation(request: Confirmation, result: MatchingResult,
54:                         snapshot: PrescriptionSnapshot, sessions: tuple[ActualSession, ...], *,
55:                         confirmation_id: str, matching_result_id: str, mapping_id: str,
56:                         answer_type: ConfirmationAnswerType, selected_session_ref: str | None,
57:                         actor: str, answered_at: datetime, provenance) -> tuple[Confirmation, MatchingResult]:
58:     if request.status is not ConfirmationStatus.REQUIRED:
59:         raise ValueError("only a REQUIRED confirmation can be answered")
60:     if result.status is not MatchingStatus.CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED or result.prescription_mapping is not None:
61:         raise ValueError("confirmation must resolve an unresolved matching result")
62:     if request.matching_result_ref != result.matching_result_id:
63:         raise ValueError("confirmation references a different matching result")
64:     if (request.prescription_snapshot_ref != snapshot.prescription_snapshot_id or
65:             result.prescription_snapshot_ref != snapshot.prescription_snapshot_id or
66:             request.candidate_session_refs != result.candidate_set):
67:         raise ValueError("confirmation snapshot or candidate set does not match")
68:     by_id = {item.session_id: item for item in sessions}
69:     if (len(by_id) != len(sessions) or set(by_id) != set(request.declared_session_refs) or
70:             any(validate_actual_session(item) for item in sessions)):
71:         raise ValueError("confirmation sessions must be the exact canonical declared set")

295:         errors.append("evaluated_at must not precede stability.evaluated_at")
296:     errors.extend(_validate(provenance_ref, ProvenanceRef, "provenance_ref"))
297:     return tuple(errors)

24: def _component_checks(snapshot: PrescriptionSnapshot, session: ActualSession) -> dict[str, bool]:
25:     planned = tuple(sorted(snapshot.components, key=lambda value: value.component_index))
26:     observed = tuple(sorted(session.components, key=lambda value: value.component_index))
27:     def compatible(expected, actual) -> bool:
28:         if expected.discipline is actual.discipline:
29:             return True
30:         return any(substitution.discipline is actual.discipline and
31:                    (substitution.environment is None or substitution.environment is actual.environment) and
32:                    (substitution.mode is None or substitution.mode is actual.mode)
33:                    for substitution in expected.allowed_substitutions)
34:     disciplines_match = len(planned) == len(observed) and all(
35:         compatible(expected, actual) for expected, actual in zip(planned, observed))
36:     return {
37:         "scheduled_window": snapshot.scheduled_window.start <= session.start <= snapshot.scheduled_window.end,
38:         "composition": session.composition is snapshot.composition,
39:         "component_cardinality": len(observed) == len(planned),
40:         "component_order": all(right.component_index == left.component_index + 1
41:                                for left, right in zip(observed, observed[1:])),
42:         "disciplines": disciplines_match,
43:     }
44: 
45: 
46: def _consecutivity(snapshot: PrescriptionSnapshot, session: ActualSession) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
47:     if snapshot.composition.value == "single":
48:         return True, None
49:     if snapshot.brick_policy.policy_id is None or snapshot.brick_policy.policy_version is None:
50:         return False, "composed session requires an explicit consecutivity policy"
51:     planned = tuple(sorted(snapshot.components, key=lambda value: value.component_index))
52:     observed = tuple(sorted(session.components, key=lambda value: value.component_index))
53:     limits = {(item.from_component_id, item.to_component_id): item.applicable_limit_minutes
54:               for item in snapshot.transitions}
55:     transitions = {(item.from_component_ref, item.to_component_ref): item
56:                    for item in session.transitions}
57:     for position, (left, right) in enumerate(zip(observed, observed[1:])):
58:         if left.start is None or left.end is None or right.start is None:
59:             return False, "component timing is incomplete"
60:         if right.start < left.end:
61:             return False, "components overlap"
62:         if len(observed) != len(planned):
63:             return False, "component sequence is not consecutive"
--- righe 73-100 ---
73:             return False, "observed transition is missing or dangling"
74:         if transition.duration_minutes is not None and transition.duration_minutes != gap:
75:             return False, "observed transition duration is incoherent"
76:     if any(item.from_component_ref not in {c.component_id for c in observed} or
77:            item.to_component_ref not in {c.component_id for c in observed}
78:            for item in session.transitions):
79:         return False, "observed transition is dangling"
80:     return True, None

=== backend/maintain_plan/ownership.py ===
FILE: backend/maintain_plan/ownership.py
--- righe 1-24 ---
1: """Normative subject ownership primitives for MAINTAIN_PLAN artifacts."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: 
6: def subject_ref_errors(value: object, *, field: str = "subject_ref") -> tuple[str, ...]:
7:     """Validate an opaque subject reference without altering its representation."""
8:     if type(value) is not str:
9:         return (f"{field} must be an explicit string",)
10:     if not value or value.isspace():
11:         return (f"{field} must be non-empty",)
12:     try:
13:         value.encode("utf-8", errors="strict")
14:     except UnicodeEncodeError:
15:         return (f"{field} must be valid UTF-8",)
16:     return ()
17: 
18: 
19: def require_subject_ref(value: object, *, field: str = "subject_ref") -> str:
20:     """Return an unchanged valid subject reference or fail closed."""
21:     errors = subject_ref_errors(value, field=field)
22:     if errors:
23:         raise ValueError(errors[0])
24:     return value

=== backend/maintain_plan/prescription_snapshot_service.py ===
FILE: backend/maintain_plan/prescription_snapshot_service.py
--- righe 2-112 ---
2: 
3: Callers must present a complete canonical prescription wrapped as an explicit
4: communication event; this boundary never looks up or infers prescription data.
5: """
6: 
7: from __future__ import annotations
8: 
9: from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
10: from typing import ContextManager, Protocol
11: 
12: from .models import PrescriptionSnapshot
13: from .serialization import deserialize_contract, serialize_contract
14: from .validators import validate_prescription
15: 
16: 
17: class PrescriptionSnapshotTransaction(Protocol):
18:     """Operations performed under one write-locked repository transaction."""
19: 
20:     def create(self, value: PrescriptionSnapshot) -> None: ...
21: 
22:     def get(self, identifier: str) -> PrescriptionSnapshot | None: ...
23: 
24:     def get_by_decision_id(
25:         self, decision_id: str
26:     ) -> tuple[PrescriptionSnapshot, ...]: ...
27: 
28: 
29: class PrescriptionSnapshotRepository(Protocol):
30:     """Repository boundary needed for atomic snapshot acquisition."""
31: 
32:     def prescription_snapshot_transaction(
33:         self,
34:     ) -> ContextManager[PrescriptionSnapshotTransaction]: ...
35: 
36: 
37: @dataclass(frozen=True)
38: class CommunicatedPrescription:
39:     """A caller's explicit declaration that this exact prescription was communicated."""
40: 
41:     snapshot: PrescriptionSnapshot
42: 
43: 
44: class PrescriptionSnapshotConflictError(ValueError):
45:     """An identifier was retried with different prescription content."""
46: 
47: 
48: def _retry_equivalent(
49:     stored: PrescriptionSnapshot,
50:     candidate: PrescriptionSnapshot,
51: ) -> bool:
52:     """Compare retry payloads while excluding their two capture timestamps."""
53:     return replace(
54:         stored,
55:         communicated_at=candidate.communicated_at,
56:         provenance=replace(
57:             stored.provenance,
58:             captured_at=candidate.provenance.captured_at,
59:         ),
60:     ) == candidate
61: 
62: 
63: class PrescriptionSnapshotService:
64:     """Validate, defensively copy, and insert a communicated prescription."""
65: 
66:     def __init__(self, repository: PrescriptionSnapshotRepository):
67:         self._repository = repository
68: 
69:     def acquire(self, communicated: CommunicatedPrescription) -> PrescriptionSnapshot:
70:         """Persist one immutable snapshot and return its canonical stored value.
71: 
72:         No legacy workout, training context, observed activity, matching result,
73:         or evaluation is accepted by this API.  Every authored value, identifier,
74:         and timestamp therefore comes solely from ``communicated.snapshot``.
75:         """
76:         if not isinstance(communicated, CommunicatedPrescription):
77:             raise TypeError("acquire requires an explicitly CommunicatedPrescription")
78: 
79:         # Codec round-tripping is the canonical defensive copy.  It recursively
80:         # rebuilds every contract container and freezes nested mappings/sets.
81:         snapshot = deserialize_contract(
82:             serialize_contract(communicated.snapshot), PrescriptionSnapshot
83:         )
84:         errors = validate_prescription(snapshot)
85:         if errors:
86:             raise ValueError("; ".join(errors))
87: 
88:         # BEGIN IMMEDIATE is acquired by this boundary before either lookup. It
89:         # serializes the decision check and insert across independent SQLite
90:         # connections without changing the append-only schema.
91:         with self._repository.prescription_snapshot_transaction() as transaction:
92:             matches = transaction.get_by_decision_id(snapshot.decision_id)
93:             if len(matches) > 1:
94:                 raise PrescriptionSnapshotConflictError(
95:                     "multiple prescription snapshots already exist for decision_id"
96:                 )
97: 
98:             existing = transaction.get(snapshot.prescription_snapshot_id)
99:             if existing is None and matches:
100:                 existing = matches[0]
101:             if existing is not None:
102:                 if _retry_equivalent(existing, snapshot):
103:                     return existing
104:                 raise PrescriptionSnapshotConflictError(
105:                     "prescription snapshot decision_id already has different content"
106:                 )
107: 
108:             transaction.create(snapshot)
109:             stored = transaction.get(snapshot.prescription_snapshot_id)
110:             if stored is None or stored != snapshot:
111:                 raise RuntimeError("persisted prescription snapshot failed canonical round-trip")
112:             return stored

151:     @contextmanager
152:     def prescription_snapshot_transaction(
153:         self,
154:     ) -> Iterator["PrescriptionSnapshotTransaction"]:
155:         """Hold SQLite's write lock for an entire snapshot acquisition."""
156:         connection = self._connect()
157:         connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
158:         try:
159:             connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
160:             yield PrescriptionSnapshotTransaction(self, connection)
161:             connection.commit()
162:         except Exception:
163:             connection.rollback()
164:             raise
165:         finally:
166:             connection.close()

168:     def get_prescription_snapshot(self, identifier: str) -> PrescriptionSnapshot | None:
169:         stored = self._get("maintain_plan_prescription_snapshots", "prescription_snapshot_id",
170:                            identifier, PrescriptionSnapshot)
171:         if stored is None:
172:             return None
173:         row, value = stored
174:         self._require_valid(validate_prescription(
175:             value, allow_legacy_subject=row["subject_ref"] is None))
176:         if (row["prescription_snapshot_id"], row["workout_id"], row["decision_id"],
177:                 row["contract_version"]) != (value.prescription_snapshot_id, value.workout_id,
178:                                              value.decision_id, value.contract_version) or \
179:                 row["subject_ref"] != value.subject_ref:
180:             raise ValueError("stored prescription snapshot metadata does not match payload")
181:         return value

184:         """Return the complete persisted snapshot set for one exact subject."""
185:         with self._connect() as connection:

189:                 "WHERE subject_ref = ? ORDER BY prescription_snapshot_id", (subject_ref,),
190:             ).fetchall()

199:         if (row["prescription_snapshot_id"], row["workout_id"], row["decision_id"],

311:     def acquire_recovery_candidate_set(
312:         self, candidate_set_id: str, value: RecoveryAssessmentCandidateSet,
313:     ) -> RecoveryAssessmentCandidateSet:
314:         """Atomically acquire one immutable candidate set for an actual session."""
315:         if type(candidate_set_id) is not str or not candidate_set_id.strip():
316:             raise ValueError("candidate_set_id must be a non-empty string")
317:         self._require_valid(validate_recovery_candidate_set(value))
318:         session_ref = value.actual_session_ref
319:         if session_ref.artifact_type != "actual-session":
320:             raise ValueError("candidate set must reference an actual session")
321:         session = self.get_actual_session(session_ref.artifact_id)
322:         if session is None:
323:             raise ValueError("candidate set references a missing actual session")
324:         if session.subject_ref != value.subject_ref:
325:             raise ValueError("candidate set session ownership is incoherent")
326:         stored_row = None
327:         stored = None
328:         with self._connect() as connection:
329:             connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
330:             connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
331:             stored_row = connection.execute(
332:                 "SELECT * FROM maintain_plan_recovery_candidate_sets "
333:                 "WHERE candidate_set_id = ?",
334:                 (candidate_set_id,),
335:             ).fetchone()
336:             if stored_row is None:
337:                 connection.execute(
338:                     "INSERT INTO maintain_plan_recovery_candidate_sets "
339:                     "(candidate_set_id, subject_ref, actual_session_ref, contract_version, "
340:                     "captured_at, evaluated_cutoff_at, payload_schema_version, payload_json) "

345:                      serialize_contract(value)),
346:                 )
347:                 return value
348:             stored = self._decode_recovery_candidate_set_row(stored_row)
349:         # Validate the existing row after releasing the write transaction.  The
350:         # validator follows the foreign key to the immutable session row.
351:         self._validate_recovery_candidate_set_row(stored_row, candidate_set_id, stored)
352:         if stored == value:
353:             return stored
354:         raise ValueError("recovery candidate set already exists with different content")

514:         self, row: sqlite3.Row, value: ReportedProblemsProjectionSnapshot,
515:     ) -> None:

550:     @contextmanager
551:     def actual_session_capture_transaction(self) -> Iterator[ActualSessionCaptureTransaction]:
552:         """Serialize append-only lookup and insertion under one write lock."""
553:         connection = self._connect()
554:         connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
555:         try:
556:             connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
557:             yield ActualSessionCaptureTransaction(self, connection)
558:             connection.commit()
559:         except Exception:
560:             connection.rollback()
561:             raise
562:         finally:
563:             connection.close()

565:     def _decode_actual_session_row(self, row: sqlite3.Row) -> ActualSession:
566:         if row["payload_schema_version"] != PAYLOAD_SCHEMA_VERSION:
567:             raise ValueError("unsupported stored MAINTAIN_PLAN payload schema version")
568:         # Migration 7 could only add the ownership column: pre-v7 payloads do
569:         # not contain subject_ref.  Some archives subsequently bound those
570:         # legacy rows in that column.  Hydrate only that precisely recognised
571:         # legacy shape; modern payloads must still match the column exactly.
572:         value = deserialize_contract(
573:             row["payload_json"], ActualSession,
574:             legacy_actual_session_subject_ref=row["subject_ref"],
575:         )
576:         self._require_valid(validate_actual_session(
577:             value, allow_legacy_subject=row["subject_ref"] is None))
578:         metadata = (value.session_id, value.start.isoformat(),
579:                     None if value.composition is None else value.composition.value,
580:                     value.contract_version)
581:         if (row["session_id"], row["start"], row["composition"],
582:                 row["contract_version"]) != metadata or row["subject_ref"] != value.subject_ref:
583:             raise ValueError("stored actual session metadata does not match payload")
584:         return value

1202:     def create_stability_evaluation(self, value: GeneralStabilityEvaluation) -> None:
1203:         """Append one canonical stability evaluation and reject semantic drift."""
1204:         self._require_valid(validate_general_stability_evaluation(value))

1206:         session_ref = value.actual_session_boundary.actual_session_ref

1208:             raise ValueError("stability evaluation must reference a prescription snapshot")

1210:             raise ValueError("stability evaluation must reference an actual session")
1211:         if value.subject_ref != value.prescription_binding.subject_ref:
1212:             raise ValueError("stability evaluation ownership is incoherent")
1213:         if value.subject_ref != value.actual_session_boundary.subject_ref:
1214:             raise ValueError("stability evaluation session ownership is incoherent")

1216:         session = self.get_actual_session(session_ref.artifact_id)

1218:             raise ValueError("stability evaluation references missing runtime artifacts")

1221:         if session.subject_ref != value.subject_ref:
1222:             raise ValueError("stability evaluation actual-session ownership is incoherent")

181: def _common_reasons(snapshot: PrescriptionSnapshot, session: ActualSession):
182:     reasons: list[CompatibilityReason] = []
183:     if not snapshot.scheduled_window.start <= session.start <= snapshot.scheduled_window.end:

191:         reasons.append(CompatibilityReason.COMPONENT_CARDINALITY)
192:     elif (tuple(item.component_index for item in planned) != tuple(range(len(planned))) or
193:           tuple(item.component_index for item in observed) != tuple(range(len(observed)))):
194:         reasons.append(CompatibilityReason.COMPONENT_ORDER)
195:     else:
196:         for expected, actual in zip(planned, observed):
197:             reasons.extend(_component_reasons(expected, actual))
198:     return planned, observed, reasons

32: def build_prescription_recovery_baseline(
33:     *, snapshot, analyzer_result, source_subject_ref, observation_ref,
34:     assessment_id, observed_at, assessed_at, captured_at, decision_ref,
35:     baseline_use_attestation_ref, assessment_provenance_ref, binding_provenance_ref,
36: ) -> PrescriptionRecoveryBaseline:
37:     """Freeze the exact analyzer output and explicit evidence used for a decision."""
38:     if type(snapshot) is not PrescriptionSnapshot:
39:         raise RecoveryBaselineCaptureError(("snapshot must be a PrescriptionSnapshot",))
40:     errors = list(validate_prescription(snapshot))
41:     errors.extend(validate_artifact_ref(observation_ref, "observation_ref"))
42:     errors.extend(validate_provenance(assessment_provenance_ref, "assessment_provenance_ref"))
43:     errors.extend(validate_provenance(binding_provenance_ref, "binding_provenance_ref"))
44:     errors.extend(validate_artifact_ref(decision_ref, "decision_ref"))
45:     errors.extend(validate_artifact_ref(baseline_use_attestation_ref, "baseline_use_attestation_ref"))
46:     if source_subject_ref != snapshot.subject_ref:
47:         errors.append("source subject must equal snapshot ownership")
48:     if errors:
49:         raise RecoveryBaselineCaptureError(errors)
50:     if type(analyzer_result) is not dict:
51:         raise RecoveryBaselineCaptureError(("analyzer_result must be an explicit dictionary",))
52:     level = analyzer_result.get("level")
53:     if level is None:
54:         raise RecoveryBaselineCaptureError(("analyzer_result.level is required",), code="INPUT_MISSING")
55:     if type(level) is not str or level not in ("LOW", "MODERATE", "HIGH", "CRITICAL", "UNKNOWN"):
56:         raise RecoveryBaselineCaptureError(("analyzer_result.level is unsupported",))
57:     if level == "UNKNOWN":
58:         category, missingness = None, CategoryMissingness.NOT_ASSESSABLE
59:         missing_fields = ("baseline_assessment.category",)
60:     else:
61:         category, missingness = RecoveryCategory(level), CategoryMissingness.NOT_MISSING
62:         missing_fields = ()
63:     try:
64:         output_json = json.dumps(analyzer_result, allow_nan=False, ensure_ascii=False,
65:                                  sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
66:     except (TypeError, ValueError) as error:
67:         raise RecoveryBaselineCaptureError(("analyzer_result must be finite JSON data",)) from error
68:     assessment = RecoveryAssessment(
69:         assessment_id=assessment_id, contract_version=STABILITY_CONTRACT_VERSION,
70:         analyzer_ref=RECOVERY_ANALYZER_REF, subject_ref=snapshot.subject_ref,
71:         observed_at=observed_at, assessed_at=assessed_at,
72:         category=category, category_missingness=missingness,
73:         evidence_refs=(observation_ref,), provenance_ref=assessment_provenance_ref,
74:         missing_fields=missing_fields,
75:     )
76:     binding = PrescriptionBaselineBinding(
77:         prescription_snapshot_ref=VersionedArtifactRef(
78:             "prescription-snapshot", snapshot.prescription_snapshot_id, snapshot.contract_version),
79:         decision_ref=decision_ref, subject_ref=snapshot.subject_ref,
80:         prescription_communicated_at=snapshot.communicated_at,
81:         baseline_assessment_ref=recovery_assessment_ref(assessment),
82:         baseline_use_attestation_ref=baseline_use_attestation_ref,
83:         provenance_ref=binding_provenance_ref,
84:     )

86:     errors = validate_recovery_baseline(value, snapshot)
87:     if errors:
88:         raise RecoveryBaselineCaptureError(errors)

30:     errors = []

51:         if execution.prescription_snapshot_ref != selected.prescription_snapshot_id:

56:         return {"$type": "frozenset", "items": encoded}
57:     if isinstance(value, dict) or hasattr(value, "items"):
58:         entries = [[_encode(key), _encode(item)] for key, item in value.items()]
59:         entries.sort(key=lambda item: json.dumps(item[0], sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")))
60:         return {"$type": "mapping", "items": entries}
61:     if value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, int, float, str)):

65: 
66: def _decode(value: Any, *, legacy_actual_session_subject_ref: str | None = None) -> Any:
67:     if value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, int, float, str)):
68:         return value
69:     if not isinstance(value, dict) or "$type" not in value:
70:         raise ValueError("untagged container in MAINTAIN_PLAN payload")
71:     kind = value["$type"]
72:     if kind == "dataclass":
73:         _require_keys(value, {"$type", "name", "fields"})
74:         try:
75:             cls = _DATACLASSES[value["name"]]
76:         except KeyError as error:
77:             raise ValueError("unknown MAINTAIN_PLAN dataclass") from error
78:         if not isinstance(value["fields"], dict):
79:             raise ValueError("invalid MAINTAIN_PLAN dataclass fields")
80:         expected_fields = {field.name for field in fields(cls)}
81:         actual_fields = set(value["fields"])
82:         legacy_subject = cls in (models.PrescriptionSnapshot, models.ActualSession) and \
83:             actual_fields == expected_fields - {"subject_ref"}
84:         if actual_fields != expected_fields and not legacy_subject:
85:             raise ValueError("MAINTAIN_PLAN dataclass fields do not match its type")
86:         decoded = {
87:             name: _decode(
88:                 item,
89:                 legacy_actual_session_subject_ref=legacy_actual_session_subject_ref,
90:             )
91:             for name, item in value["fields"].items()
92:         }
93:         if legacy_subject:
94:             decoded["subject_ref"] = (
95:                 legacy_actual_session_subject_ref
96:                 if cls is models.ActualSession
97:                 else None
98:             )
99:         return cls(**decoded)
100:     if kind == "enum":
101:         _require_keys(value, {"$type", "name", "value"})
102:         try:
103:             return _ENUMS[value["name"]](value["value"])
104:         except (KeyError, ValueError) as error:
105:             raise ValueError("unknown MAINTAIN_PLAN enum or value") from error
106:     if kind == "datetime":
107:         _require_keys(value, {"$type", "value"})
108:         try:
109:             return datetime.fromisoformat(value["value"])
110:         except (TypeError, ValueError) as error:
111:             raise ValueError("invalid MAINTAIN_PLAN datetime") from error
112:     if kind == "tuple":
113:         _require_keys(value, {"$type", "items"})
114:         if not isinstance(value["items"], list):
115:             raise ValueError("invalid MAINTAIN_PLAN tuple")
116:         return tuple(_decode(
117:             item,
118:             legacy_actual_session_subject_ref=legacy_actual_session_subject_ref,
119:         ) for item in value["items"])
120:     if kind == "frozenset":
121:         _require_keys(value, {"$type", "items"})
122:         if not isinstance(value["items"], list):
123:             raise ValueError("invalid MAINTAIN_PLAN frozenset")
124:         return frozenset(_decode(
125:             item,
126:             legacy_actual_session_subject_ref=legacy_actual_session_subject_ref,
127:         ) for item in value["items"])
128:     if kind == "mapping":
129:         _require_keys(value, {"$type", "items"})
130:         if not isinstance(value["items"], list):
131:             raise ValueError("invalid MAINTAIN_PLAN mapping")
132:         result = {}
133:         for entry in value["items"]:
134:             if not isinstance(entry, list) or len(entry) != 2:
135:                 raise ValueError("invalid MAINTAIN_PLAN mapping entry")
136:             key = _decode(
137:                 entry[0],
138:                 legacy_actual_session_subject_ref=legacy_actual_session_subject_ref,
139:             )
140:             if key in result:
141:                 raise ValueError("duplicate MAINTAIN_PLAN mapping key")
142:             result[key] = _decode(
143:                 entry[1],
144:                 legacy_actual_session_subject_ref=legacy_actual_session_subject_ref,
145:             )
146:         return result
147:     raise ValueError(f"unknown MAINTAIN_PLAN payload tag: {kind}")
148: 
149: 
150: def serialize_contract(value: Any) -> str:
151:     """Return canonical UTF-8-compatible JSON with an explicit envelope version."""
152:     envelope = {"payload_schema_version": PAYLOAD_SCHEMA_VERSION, "payload": _encode(value)}
153:     return json.dumps(
154:         envelope, allow_nan=False, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")
155:     )
156: 
157: 
158: def deserialize_contract(
159:     payload: str,
160:     expected_type: type[Any],
161:     *,
162:     legacy_actual_session_subject_ref: str | None = None,
163: ) -> Any:
164:     """Rebuild a canonical immutable contract object, rejecting wrong versions/types."""
165:     try:
166:         envelope = json.loads(
167:             payload,
168:             parse_constant=lambda value: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
169:                 ValueError(f"invalid JSON constant: {value}")
170:             ),
171:         )
172:     except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as error:
173:         raise ValueError("invalid MAINTAIN_PLAN JSON payload") from error
174:     if not isinstance(envelope, dict) or set(envelope) != {"payload_schema_version", "payload"}:
175:         raise ValueError("invalid MAINTAIN_PLAN payload envelope")
176:     if envelope.get("payload_schema_version") != PAYLOAD_SCHEMA_VERSION:
177:         raise ValueError("unsupported MAINTAIN_PLAN payload schema version")
178:     value = _decode(
179:         envelope["payload"],
180:         legacy_actual_session_subject_ref=legacy_actual_session_subject_ref,
181:     )
182:     if not isinstance(value, expected_type):
183:         raise ValueError(f"expected {expected_type.__name__} payload")
184:     return value

19: @dataclass(frozen=True)
20: class SessionPage:
21:     items: tuple[Any, ...]
22:     page: int
23:     pages: int

25: 
26: def list_sessions(repository, subject_ref: str, *, page: int = 1, year: int | None = None,
27:                   month: int | None = None, sport: str | None = None) -> SessionPage:
28:     """Filter and paginate without mutating the archive."""
29:     if page < 1: raise ValueError("page must be positive")
30:     items = sorted(
31:         repository.list_actual_sessions(subject_ref),
32:         key=lambda session: session.start,
33:         reverse=True,
34:     )
35:     def discipline(s):
36:         return {getattr(c.discipline, "value", c.discipline) for c in s.components}
37:     items = [s for s in items if (year is None or s.start.year == year)
38:              and (month is None or s.start.month == month)
39:              and (sport is None or sport.upper() in discipline(s))]
40:     total = len(items); pages = max(1, (total + PAGE_SIZE - 1) // PAGE_SIZE)
41:     if page > pages: return SessionPage((), page, pages, total)
42:     start = (page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE
43:     return SessionPage(tuple(items[start:start + PAGE_SIZE]), page, pages, total)

204:     temporary.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
205:     temporary.replace(path)
206:     return data
207: 
208: def load_imported_plan(storage_path: str | Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
209:     path = Path(storage_path)
210:     if not path.exists(): return None
211:     return validate_imported_plan(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

24: def _duplicates(values: Iterable[str]) -> set[str]:
25:     counts = Counter(values)
26:     return {value for value, count in counts.items() if count > 1}
27: 
28: 
29: def validate_policy_ref(policy: PolicyRef, *, required: bool = False) -> tuple[str, ...]:
30:     complete = policy.policy_id is not None and policy.policy_version is not None
31:     empty = policy.policy_id is None and policy.policy_version is None
32:     if not (complete or empty):
33:         return ("policy_id and policy_version must be both set or both null",)
34:     if required and not complete:
35:         return ("policy is required",)
36:     if complete and (not policy.policy_id or not policy.policy_version):
37:         return ("policy_id and policy_version must be non-empty when set",)
38:     return ()

398:     """Resolve every mapping reference through its complete canonical parent chain."""

633: 
634: def expected_coverage(snapshot: PrescriptionSnapshot) -> CoverageStatus:
635:     required = [component for component in snapshot.components
636:                 if component.requiredness is Requiredness.REQUIRED]
637:     if not required:
638:         return CoverageStatus.NO_REQUIRED_COMPONENTS
639:     supported = sum(r.support_status is SupportStatus.SUPPORTED for r in required)
640:     if supported == len(required):
641:         return CoverageStatus.FULLY_SUPPORTED
642:     if supported == 0:
643:         return CoverageStatus.UNSUPPORTED
644:     return CoverageStatus.PARTIALLY_UNSUPPORTED
645: 
646: 
647: def validate_execution_evaluation(
648:     evaluation: ExecutionEvaluation,
649:     mapping: PrescriptionMapping,
650:     snapshot: PrescriptionSnapshot,
651:     session: ActualSession | None = None,
652: ) -> tuple[str, ...]:
653:     errors = list(validate_mapping(mapping) if session is None else
654:                   validate_mapping_ownership(mapping, snapshot, session))
655:     if evaluation.prescription_mapping_ref != mapping.mapping_id:
656:         errors.append("execution evaluation must reference the identified canonical mapping")
657:     if evaluation.prescription_snapshot_ref != mapping.prescription_snapshot_ref or evaluation.actual_session_ref != mapping.actual_session_ref:
658:         errors.append("execution evaluation snapshot/session references must match mapping")
659:     if snapshot.prescription_snapshot_id != mapping.prescription_snapshot_ref:
660:         errors.append("authoritative prescription snapshot must match canonical mapping")
661:     if _duplicates(r.component_result_id for r in evaluation.component_results):
662:         errors.append("component_result_id must be unique in execution evaluation")
663:     dose_ids = [r.dose.dose_result_id for r in evaluation.component_results if r.dose]
664:     if evaluation.dose_aggregate:
665:         dose_ids.append(evaluation.dose_aggregate.dose_result_id)
666:     if _duplicates(dose_ids):
667:         errors.append("dose_result_id must be unique in execution evaluation")

18: class StoreTests(unittest.TestCase):

22:         self.archive = Path(self.temp.name) / "archive.sqlite3"

24:         self.repository = SimpleNamespace(
25:             database_path=self.archive,
26:             get_actual_session=lambda key: SimpleNamespace(subject_ref="a") if key == "s" else None,
27:         )

31:         self.assertFalse(analysis_database_path(self.repository).exists())
32: 
33:     def test_save_reload_and_preserve_previous_version_and_archive(self):
34:         before = self.archive.read_bytes()
35:         save_manual_analysis(self.repository, "a", "s", "Prima analisi")
36:         save_manual_analysis(self.repository, "a", "s", "Analisi aggiornata")
37:         saved = load_manual_analysis(self.repository, "a", "s")
38:         self.assertEqual(saved["text"], "Analisi aggiornata")
39:         self.assertEqual(saved["origin"], "chatgpt_manual")
40:         with sqlite3.connect(analysis_database_path(self.repository)) as db:
41:             self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM manual_session_analyses").fetchone()[0], 2)
42:         self.assertEqual(before, self.archive.read_bytes())

156: 
157: def _concurrent_acquire(path, snapshots):
158:     MaintainPlanRepository(path)
159:     barrier = Barrier(len(snapshots))
160: 
161:     def acquire(snapshot):
162:         snapshot_service = PrescriptionSnapshotService(MaintainPlanRepository(path))
163:         barrier.wait()
164:         return snapshot_service.acquire(CommunicatedPrescription(snapshot))
165: 
166:     with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(snapshots)) as executor:
167:         futures = [executor.submit(acquire, snapshot) for snapshot in snapshots]
168:         results = []
169:         errors = []
170:         for future in futures:
171:             try:
172:                 results.append(future.result(timeout=10))
173:             except Exception as error:  # assertions below inspect concurrent outcomes
174:                 errors.append(error)
175:     return results, errors
176:

115:     return {
116:         "snapshot": RUN_PRESCRIPTION,
117:         "analyzer_result": analyzer,
118:         "source_subject_ref": value.binding.subject_ref,
119:         "observation_ref": value.assessment.evidence_refs[0],
120:         "assessment_id": value.assessment.assessment_id,
121:         "observed_at": value.assessment.observed_at,
122:         "assessed_at": value.assessment.assessed_at,
123:         "captured_at": captured or value.captured_at,
124:         "decision_ref": value.binding.decision_ref,
125:         "baseline_use_attestation_ref": value.binding.baseline_use_attestation_ref,
126:         "assessment_provenance_ref": value.assessment.provenance_ref,
127:         "binding_provenance_ref": value.binding.provenance_ref,

217: def test_independent_connections_serialize_equivalent_batches(tmp_path):
218:     cfg = config(tmp_path / "sessions.db")
219:     # Migrations happen before the competing capture calls, exactly as they do
220:     # during application startup; each capture still opens its own connection.
221:     MaintainPlanRepository(cfg.maintain_plan_database_path)
222:     batch = [activity()]
223:     def capture():
224:         return RuntimeActualSessionCapture().capture(
225:             runtime_config=cfg, athlete={"source_id": "athlete"},
226:             garmin_training_history=batch, normalized_at=NOW)
227:     with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
228:         results = list(pool.map(lambda _: capture(), range(2)))
229:     assert sum(len(item.created) for item in results) == 1
230:     assert sum(len(item.reused) for item in results) == 1

10: def _session(i, sport="RUN"):
11:     component = SimpleNamespace(discipline=Discipline.RUN if sport == "RUN" else Discipline.BIKE, quantity_primary_metric="distance",
12:                                missing_fields=(), secondary_metrics=(), blocks=(), quantity_observation=None,
13:                                start=None, end=None)
14:     return SimpleNamespace(session_id=str(i), subject_ref="a", start=datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
15:                            end=None, components=(component,), source_activities=(), missing_fields=(), athlete_feedback=None)
16: 
17: 
18: def test_pagination_and_filtering():
19:     class Repo:
20:         def list_actual_sessions(self, subject): return tuple(_session(i, "BIKE" if i == 1 else "RUN") for i in range(51))
21:     page = list_sessions(Repo(), "a", page=1, sport="RUN")
22:     assert page.pages == 1 and len(page.items) == 50

25: def test_render_contains_navigation_export_and_import_preview():
26:     html = render_page("a", sessions=(_session(1),), selected_session=_session(1), page=1, pages=2)
27:     assert "Esamina" in html and "Apri ChatGPT" in html
28:     assert "chatgpt.com/?q=" in html and "Importa piano JSON" in html

34: def test_import_persists_and_reloads_after_restart(tmp_path):
35:     value = {"sessions": [{"date": "2025-01-01T10:00:00Z", "sport": "RUN"}]}
36:     persist_imported_plan(value, tmp_path / "plan.json")
37:     assert load_imported_plan(tmp_path / "plan.json") == value

39: def test_export_prompt_contains_session_history_prescription_and_feedback():
40:     session = _session(1); session.athlete_feedback = {"rpe": 8, "comment": "duro"}
41:     prompt = build_export_prompt(session, [session], {"id": "p1"})
42:     assert '"session_id": "1"' in prompt and "storico_recente" in prompt
43:     assert '"id": "p1"' in prompt and "duro" in prompt and "8" in prompt

45: from backend.maintain_plan.session_flow import preview_text_plan, text_to_plan
46: 
47: 
48: def test_text_plan_is_converted_to_internal_sessions():
49:     plan = text_to_plan(
50:         "06/10/2026 - corsa facile per 45 minuti\n"
51:         "2026-10-08 - bici con 6 ripetute"
52:     )
53:     assert [item["sport"] for item in plan["sessions"]] == ["RUN", "BIKE"]
54:     assert preview_text_plan("2026-10-06 - nuoto tecnico")[0]["sport"] == "SWIM"

56: 
57: def test_text_plan_reports_missing_date():
58:     try:
59:         text_to_plan("corsa facile senza data")
60:     except ValueError as error:
61:         assert "data" in str(error)
62:     else:
63:         raise AssertionError(
64:             "La descrizione senza data deve chiedere un chiarimento"
65:         )
